### app concurrent RSS delta 100 connections

Reports RSS growth across the concurrent request burst so sustained-load memory trends are visible in router benchmark output.

RSS delta rows only rank adapters that expose server child process RSS; adapters without measurable server child RSS are reported as unsupported, and rows with negative RSS samples are treated as contaminated and excluded from this ranking.

Same-core mreact variant noise floor: +6% spread. Treat smaller cross-framework gaps in this case as inconclusive.

| rank | framework | case | value | diff vs 1st | unit |
| ---: | --- | --- | ---: | ---: | --- |
| 1 | tanstack-start-solid | app concurrent RSS delta 100 connections | 12288 | best | bytes |
| 2 | qwik-city | app concurrent RSS delta 100 connections | 151552 | +1133.33% | bytes |
| 3 | **mreact-app-router+mreact react-compat** | app concurrent RSS delta 100 connections | 23085056 | +187766.67% | bytes |
